````text
Now I have key info. Let me check the critical boundary logic for n_min=4 with batch_size=4.

From alpha_eff.py line 99-100:
```python
if n_min > n:
    n_min = n
```

So when n_min=4 and batch_size=4, the batch has 4 samples. When we call find_optimal_b with n_min=4:
- n = len(q_scores) = batch_size = 4
- n_min > n is False (4 > 4 is False), so n_min stays 4


- The constraint ESS(b*) >= 4 can't be satisfied since ESS(0) = 4 (uniform weights) and any b > 0 reduces ESS below 4, so the algorithm falls back to b* = 0.
- For the ess_only boundary case, the default n_min is 2 (not 4), so the behavior differs from the nmin4 experiment.

Looking at the nmin4_seed42 script more carefully, it includes inline evaluation with eval_data_path and eval_steps, whereas the nmin1_seed42 registry spec doesn't have these parameters. The tau sweep commands also lack inline evaluation.

Checking the actual nmin4 registry entry, the planned command doesn't include eval_data_path or eval_steps either—there's a mismatch between what's in the script and what's in the registry. The ess_only_seed42 command follows the same pattern without inline eval.

Since the tau sweep experiments and nmin1 both skipped inline eval, and recent runs like grid_b0_5_seed456 ran into OOM and timeout issues with eval enabled, it makes sense to continue without inline eval for the next batch and rely on post-hoc evaluation instead.

Now looking at the n_min clamping logic—if the batch has fewer samples than n_min, it gets clamped down to match the actual batch size.  With batch_size=4 and n_min=4, complete batches satisfy the ESS constraint at b=0 (uniform weights give ESS=n=4), but any b>0 reduces ESS below the threshold when q_scores vary, forcing b*=0 in practice.

Now I'm looking at the actual implementation of the b_star selection logic in the find_optimal_b function.

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Convergence-theoretic adaptive contamination reweighting.\\n\\nImplements the effective authenticity rate α_eff(b) and related quantities\\nfor importance-weighted pretraining on contaminated corpora.\\n\\nKey idea: given a detector that outputs per-sample synthetic probability q_i,\\nassign weight w_i = (1 − q_i)^b. The parameter b controls the bias-variance\\ntradeoff: higher b downweights suspected synthetic data more aggressively\\nbut reduces effective sample size (ESS).\\n\\nCore quantities:\\n  α_eff(b) = Σ (1−q_i)^{b+1} / Σ (1−q_i)^b    — effective authenticity rate\\n  ESS(b)   = (Σ w_i)² / Σ w_i²                  — effective sample size\\n  b*       = inf{b ≥ 0 : α_eff(b) ≥ τ} s.t. ESS(b*) ≥ n_min\\n\\nReferences:\\n  - arXiv 2602.16065 (convergence bounds for weighted pretraining)\\n  - Drayson et al., EMNLP 2025 (grid-search weighting baseline)\\n\\nInputs:  q_scores ∈ [0,1]^N (synthetic probabilities from detector)\\nOutputs: weights w ∈ R_+^N (normalized to sum to N)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport math\\nimport torch\\n\\n\\ndef compute_alpha_eff(q_scores: torch.Tensor, b: float) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Effective authenticity rate α_eff(b) = Σ (1−q)^{b+1} / Σ (1−q)^b.\\n\\n    Properties:\\n      α_eff(0) = 1 − ρ̂  (unweighted authenticity)\\n      α_eff(b) → 1 as b → ∞\\n      α_eff(b) is monotonically non-decreasing in b\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    q = q_scores.clamp(0.0, 1.0).double()\\n    one_minus_q = 1.0 - q\\n\\n    if b == 0.0:\\n        return float(one_minus_q.mean())\\n\\n    log_omq = torch.log(one_minus_q.clamp(min=1e-30))\\n    log_w = b * log_omq\\n    log_w_shift = log_w - log_w.max()\\n    w = torch.exp(log_w_shift)\\n\\n    numerator = (w * one_minus_q).sum()\\n    denominator = w.sum()\\n\\n    if denominator < 1e-30:\\n        return 1.0\\n\\n    return float(numerator / denominator)\\n\\n\\ndef compute_ess(q_scores: torch.Tensor, b: float) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Effective sample size ESS(b) = (Σ w_i)² / Σ w_i².\\n\\n    ESS = N when b=0 (uniform weights), decreases as b increases.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if b == 0.0:\\n        return float(q_scores.numel())\\n\\n    q = q_scores.clamp(0.0, 1.0).double()\\n    one_minus_q = 1.0 - q\\n\\n    log_omq = torch.log(one_minus_q.clamp(min=1e-30))\\n    log_w = b * log_omq\\n    log_w_shift = log_w - log_w.max()\\n    w = torch.exp(log_w_shift)\\n\\n    sum_w = w.sum()\\n    sum_w2 = (w * w).sum()\\n\\n    if sum_w2 < 1e-30:\\n        return 0.0\\n\\n    return float((sum_w * sum_w) / sum_w2)\\n\\n\\ndef find_optimal_b(\\n    q_scores: torch.Tensor,\\n    tau: float,\\n    n_min: int,\\n    b_max: float = 20.0,\\n    resolution: int = 1000,\\n) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Find b* = inf{b ≥ 0 : α_eff(b) ≥ τ} subject to ESS(b*) ≥ n_min.\\n\\n    When the batch is clean enough (ρ̂ < 1 − τ), returns b=0 (uniform).\\n    When the ESS constraint conflicts with the α_eff threshold, returns\\n    b* = argmax_b α_eff(b) s.t. ESS(b) ≥ n_min.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    alpha_0 = compute_alpha_eff(q_scores, 0.0)\\n    if alpha_0 >= tau:\\n        return 0.0\\n\\n    n = q_scores.numel()\\n    if n_min > n:\\n        n_min = n\\n\\n    # Phase 1: grid search to find the feasible region\\n    grid = torch.linspace(0.0, b_max, resolution + 1).tolist()\\n\\n    best_feasible_b = 0.0\\n    best_feasible_alpha = alpha_0\\n    threshold_b = None\\n\\n    for b_val in grid:\\n        ess = compute_ess(q_scores, b_val)\\n        if ess < n_min:\\n            break\\n\\n        alpha = compute_alpha_eff(q_scores, b_val)\\n\\n        if alpha > best_feasible_alpha:\\n            best_feasible_alpha = alpha\\n            best_feasible_b = b_val\\n\\n        if alpha >= tau and threshold_b is None:\\n            threshold_b = b_val\\n\\n    if threshold_b is not None:\\n        # Phase 2: refine with bisection\\n        lo = max(0.0, threshold_b - b_max / resolution)\\n        hi = threshold_b\\n        for _ in range(50):\\n            mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0\\n            if compute_alpha_eff(q_scores, mid) >= tau:\\n                hi = mid\\n            else:\\n                lo = mid\\n        b_star = hi\\n        if compute_ess(q_scores, b_star) >= n_min:\\n            return b_star\\n\\n    return best_feasible_b\\n\\n\\n\\ndef find_ess_only_b(\\n    q_scores: torch.Tensor,\\n    n_min: int,\\n    b_max: float = 20.0,\\n    tol: float = 0.01,\\n) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Find b* = max{b : ESS(b) >= n_min} (ESS-only, no alpha_eff constraint).\\n\\n    ESS(b) is monotonically non-increasing in b, so binary search works.\\n    Returns b_max if ESS(b_max) >= n_min; returns 0.0 if ESS(0) < n_min.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if compute_ess(q_scores, 0.0) < n_min:\\n        return 0.0\\n\\n    if compute_ess(q_scores, b_max) >= n_min:\\n        return b_max\\n\\n    lo, hi = 0.0, b_max\\n    while hi - lo > tol:\\n        mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0\\n        if compute_ess(q_scores, mid) >= n_min:\\n            lo = mid\\n        else:\\n            hi = mid\\n    return lo\\n\\ndef compute_sample_weights(q_scores: torch.Tensor, b: float) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Per-sample weights w_i = (1−q_i)^b, normalized to sum to N.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    n = q_scores.numel()\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.45}}"}

ESS(b*) ≥ n_min\\\\n\\\\nReferences:\\\\n  - arXiv 2602.16065 (convergence bounds for weighted pretraining)\\\\n  - Drayson et al., EMNLP 2025 (grid-search weighting baseline)\\\\n\\\\nInputs:  q_scores ∈ [0,1]^N (synthetic probabilities from detector)\\\\nOutputs: weights w ∈ R_+^N (normalized to sum to N)\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport torch\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_alpha_eff(q_scores: torch.Tensor, b: float) -> float:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Effective authenticity rate α_eff(b) = Σ (1−q)^{b+1} / Σ (1−q)^b.\\\\n\\\\n    Properties:\\\\n      α_eff(0) = 1 − ρ̂  (unweighted authenticity)\\\\n      α_eff(b) → 1 as b → ∞\\\\n      α_eff(b) is monotonically non-decreasing in b\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    q = q_scores.clamp(0.0, 1.0).double()\\\\n    one_minus_q = 1.0 - q\\\\n\\\\n    if b == 0.0:\\\\n        return float(one_minus_q.mean())\\\\n\\\\n    log_omq = torch.log(one_minus_q.clamp(min=1e-30))\\\\n    log_w = b * log_omq\\\\n    log_w_shift = log_w - log_w.max()\\\\n    w = torch.exp(log_w_shift)\\\\n\\\\n    numerator = (w * one_minus_q).sum()\\\\n    denominator = w.sum()\\\\n\\\\n    if denominator < 1e-30:\\\\n        return 1.0\\\\n\\\\n    return float(numerator / denominator)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_ess(q_scores: torch.Tensor, b: float) -> float:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Effective sample size ESS(b) = (Σ w_i)² / Σ w_i².\\\\n\\\\n    ESS = N when b=0 (uniform weights), decreases as b increases.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if b == 0.0:\\\\n        return float(q_scores.numel())\\\\n\\\\n    q = q_scores.clamp(0.0, 1.0).double()\\\\n    one_minus_q = 1.0 - q\\\\n\\\\n    log_omq = torch.log(one_minus_q.clamp(min=1e-30))\\\\n    log_w = b * log_omq\\\\n    log_w_shift = log_w - log_w.max()\\\\n    w = torch.exp(log_w_shift)\\\\n\\\\n    sum_w = w.sum()\\\\n    sum_w2 = (w * w).sum()\\\\n\\\\n    if sum_w2 < 1e-30:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n\\\\n    return float((sum_w * sum_w) / sum_w2)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef find_optimal_b(\\\\n    q_scores: torch.Tensor,\\\\n    tau: float,\\\\n    n_min: int,\\\\n    b_max: float = 20.0,\\\\n    resolution: int = 1000,\\\\n) -> float:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Find b* = inf{b ≥ 0 : α_eff(b) ≥ τ} subject to ESS(b*) ≥ n_min.\\\\n\\\\n    When the batch is clean enough (ρ̂ < 1 − τ), returns b=0 (uniform).\\\\n    When the ESS constraint conflicts with the α_eff threshold, returns\\\\n    b* = argmax_b α_eff(b) s.t. ESS(b) ≥ n_min.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    alpha_0 = compute_alpha_eff(q_scores, 0.0)\\\\n    if alpha_0 >= tau:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n\\\\n    n = q_scores.numel()\\\\n    if n_min > n:\\\\n        n_min = n\\\\n\\\\n    # Phase 1: grid search to find the feasible region\\\\n    grid = torch.linspace(0.0, b_max, resolution + 1).tolist()\\\\n\\\\n    best_feasible_b = 0.0\\\\n    best_feasible_alpha = alpha_0\\\\n    threshold_b = None\\\\n\\\\n    for b_val in grid:\\\\n        ess = compute_ess(q_scores, b_val)\\\\n        if ess < n_min:\\\\n            break\\\\n\\\\n        alpha = compute_alpha_eff(q_scores, b_val)\\\\n\\\\n        if alpha > best_feasible_alpha:\\\\n            best_feasible_alpha = alpha\\\\n            best_feasible_b = b_val\\\\n\\\\n        if alpha >= tau and threshold_b is None:\\\\n            threshold_b = b_val\\\\n\\\\n    if threshold_b is not None:\\\\n        # Phase 2: refine with bisection\\\\n        lo = max(0.0, threshold_b - b_max / resolution)\\\\n        hi = threshold_b\\\\n        for _ in range(50):\\\\n            mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0\\\\n            if compute_alpha_eff(q_scores, mid) >= tau:\\\\n                hi = mid\\\\n            else:\\\\n                lo = mid\\\\n        b_star = hi\\\\n        if compute_ess(q_scores, b_star) >= n_min:\\\\n            return b_star\\\\n\\\\n    return best_feasible_b\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef find_ess_only_b(\\\\n    q_scores: torch.Tensor,\\\\n    n_min: int,\\\\n    b_max: float = 20.0,\\\\n    tol: float = 0.01,\\\\n) -> float:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Find b* = max{b : ESS(b) >= n_min} (ESS-only, no alpha_eff constraint).\\\\n\\\\n    ESS(b) is monotonically non-increasing in b, so binary search works.\\\\n    Returns b_max if ESS(b_max) >= n_min; returns 0.0 if ESS(0) < n_min.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if compute_ess(q_scores, 0.0) < n_min:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n\\\\n    if compute_ess(q_scores, b_max) >= n_min:\\\\n        return b_max\\\\n\\\\n    lo, hi = 0.0, b_max\\\\n    while hi - lo > tol:\\\\n        mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0\\\\n        if compute_ess(q_scores, mid) >= n_min:\\\\n            lo = mid\\\\n        else:\\\\n            hi = mid\\\\n    return lo\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_sample_weights(q_scores: torch.Tensor, b: float) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Per-sample weights w_i = (1−q_i)^b, normalized to sum to N.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    n = q_scores.numel()\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.45}}\"}",

Now I have everything I need. Let me analyze the n_min=4 boundary condition:

In `find_optimal_b`:
1. First checks `alpha_0 = compute_alpha_eff(q_scores, 0.0)`. If alpha_0 >= tau, returns b=0.0.
2. If alpha_0 < tau, does grid search. For each b_val, computes ESS. If ESS < n_min, breaks.
3. When n_min=4 and batch_size=4: ESS(0)=4 >= 4 ✓, but for any b>0, ESS < 4 (assuming q_scores are not all identical). 

The grid search terminates immediately when it hits the first b>0 value, leaving b*=0 as the only feasible solution. Whether the batch starts clean enough to skip the search or the search fails to find any valid b>0, the result is the same: uniform weighting across all batches. This confirms the hypothesis through the actual code path.

Now I'm setting up the experiment commands to test this behavior.

For the first run with n_min=4 and seed 42, I'm configuring the training script with adaptive weighting, setting the contamination ratio to 0.4 and tau threshold to 0.8. The evaluation will run every 500 steps on the holdout set.

For the second experiment, I'm using the same setup but switching to ess_only weighting with seed 123.

I notice that ess_only doesn't actually use the tau parameter, but including it won't cause issues since the code only applies tau for the adaptive method. I'll keep it for consistency across experiments.

Before proceeding, I should check if there's already an output directory for nmin4_seed42 that needs to be cleared out.

  "content": "# 下一批实验预准备报告\n\n生成时间: 2026-05-15 ~15:00 UTC\n\n## 1. nmin4_seed42（优先级最高，cuda:1）\n\n### 假设\nn_min=4 (=batch_size) 强制 b*→0，退化为 uniform-equivalent PPL (~17.69)\n\n### 代码验证：n_min=4 边界行为\n\n`weighting/alpha_eff.py` 中 `find_optimal_b` 逻辑：\n1. ESS(0) = n = batch_size = 4 = n_min → 满足约束\n2. 对任何 b>0，ESS(b) < 4（只要 q_scores 非全等）→ 不满足约束\n3. grid search 在第一个 b>0 就 break，返回 best_feasible_b = 0.0\n\n**结论：n_min=4 在所有非退化 batch 上 b*=0，等价于 uniform weighting。** 代码无 bug，边界行为正确。\n\n唯一例外：若某 batch 的 q_scores 全部相同（实际概率极低），ESS 恒等于 n，但此时 alpha_eff 也不随 b 变化。\n\n### 训练命令\n\n```bash\nbash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/provenance_weight_training && WANDB_MODE=disabled python training/pretrain_weighted.py --data_path data/scored_data.jsonl --output_dir output/models/nmin4_seed42 --weighting_method adaptive --n_min 4 --model_name models/Qwen/Qwen3-0.6B --num_train_tokens 200000000 --batch_size 4 --gradient_accumulation_steps 4 --max_length 2048 --learning_rate 5e-5 --seed 42 --logging_steps 10 --save_steps 500 --save_total_limit 2 --save_only_model --contamination_ratio 0.4 --tau 0.8 --bf16\"\n```\n\n> **注意：** 服务器上 `run_nmin4_seed42.sh` 包含 `--eval_data_path data/human/eval_holdout.jsonl --eval_steps 500`（inline eval），但 registry planned_command 和当前 tau sweep 实验都不含 inline eval。建议**不加 inline eval**，训练后用 post-hoc eval，与其他实验保持一致且避免 eval overhead (~40min/round)。\n\n### Registry 状态\n已存在 `nmin4_seed42`，status=PREPARING。提交时更新 spec 即可。\n\n---\n\n## 2. ess_only_seed123（Claim 1 最大缺口，cuda:0 或空闲 GPU）\n\n### 假设\nESS-only baseline (max-intervention), seed=123 复现。参考 ess_only_seed42: PPL=17.73, b_star_mean=3.51\n\n### 训练命令\n\n```bash\nbash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/provenance_weight_training && WANDB_MODE=disabled python training/pretrain_weighted.py --data_path data/scored_data.jsonl --output_dir output/models/ess_only_seed123 --weighting_method ess_only --model_name models/Qwen/Qwen3-0.6B --num_train_tokens 200000000 --batch_size 4 --gradient_accumulation_steps 4 --max_length 2048 --learning_rate 5e-5 --seed 123 --logging_steps 10 --save_steps 500 --save_total_limit 2 --save_only_model --contamination_ratio 0.4 --tau 0.8 --bf16\"\n```\n\n> `--tau 0.8` 对 ess_only 方法无实际作用（find_ess_only_b 不使用 tau），保留仅为命令一致性。n_min 未指定，默认 max(2, 4//2) = 2，与 ess_only_seed42 一致。\n\n### Registry 状态\n尚未创建，需要新建 registry entry。\n\n---\n\n## 3. 参数对比表\n\n| 参数 | nmin4_seed42 | ess_only_seed123 | 参考: nmin1_seed42 | 参考: ess_only_seed42 |\n|------|-------------|------------------|--------------------|-----------------------|\n| weighting_method | adaptive | ess_only | adaptive | ess_only |\n| n_min | **4** | 2 (default) | **1** | 2 (default) |\n| tau | 0.8 | 0.8 (unused) | 0.8 | 0.8 (unused) |\n| seed | 42 | **123** | 42 | 42 |\n| output_dir | nmin4_seed42 | ess_only_seed123 | nmin1_seed42 | ess_only_seed42 |\n| batch_size | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 |\n| grad_accum | 4 | 4 | 4 | 4 |\n| tokens | 200M | 200M | 200M | 200M |\n| lr | 5e-5 | 5e-5 | 5e-5 | 5e-5 |\n| bf16 | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ | ✓ |\n| inline eval | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ |\n\n---\n\n## 4. 文件存在性验证\n\n| 文件 | 状态 | 大小 |\n|------|------|------|\n| training/pretrain_weighted.py | ✓ 存在 | — |\n| data/scored_data.jsonl | ✓ 存在 | 975MB |\n| data/human/eval_holdout.jsonl | ✓ 存在 | 238MB |\n| models/Qwen/Qwen3-0.6B/config.json | ✓ 存在 | 726B |\n| output/models/nmin4_seed42 | ✗ 不存在（干净） | — |\n| output/models/ess_only_seed123 | ✗ 不存在（干净） | — |\n| run_nmin4_seed42.sh | ✓ 存在（含 inline eval） | — |\n\n---\n\n## 5. 预估运行时间\n\n参考 nmin1_seed42 (4h50m) 和 ess_only_seed42 (3h37m)。nmin4 由于 b*=0（无 weight 计算开销），预计 ~3.5-4h。ess_only_seed123 预计 ~3.5-4h。\n\n## 6. 需要主 Agent 决策的事项\n\n1. **nmin4 inline eval**：服务器上的 run_nmin4_seed42.sh 含 inline eval 参数，但其他同期实验（tau sweep、nmin1）都不含。建议统一不加 inline eval，post-hoc eval。如果想加，需注意 eval overhead。\n2. **GPU 分配**：nmin4 计划用 cuda:1（tau_05 当前占用），ess_only_seed123 需分配空闲 GPU。4 个 tau sweep 完成后有 cuda:0/1/2/3 全部可用。\n3. **ess_only_seed123 的 registry entry** 需要新建。\n",
